And \"doing nothing scores 0.\" So the total cost framework is: lost revenue + extra replica costs + wasted mitigation costs ($10,000 each).\n\nSince I didn't apply any mitigations to services that didn't need them (all the services I targeted had error rates above 1%), the wasted mitigation cost should be $0. I should have known better than to restart a service at capacity.\n\n2. **Restarting auth-svc when it depends on thumbnails-svc (still broken)** - the failures recurred immediately after restart. I should have fixed the dependency first.\n\n3. **Not focusing on the root cause chain** - payments-db → pricing-svc → stock-svc/thumbnails-svc → mobile-api.
